## See what to fix first

Layzr points to the first fixes most likely to improve trust, visibility, and conversion.

Find the blocker

Pinpoints the section that is slowing the page down or making the offer harder to understand.

Fix the first screen

Shows where the headline, CTA, or visual hierarchy is making visitors work too hard too early.

Sharpen the message

Flags vague headlines, weak copy, and missing context that keep the page from landing cleanly.

Recover visibility

Highlights SEO and discovery issues that make the page harder to find in search and prompts.

Strengthen trust

Shows where the page is missing proof, clarity, or confidence cues that help people move forward.

Prioritize the next move

Turns the findings into a short list of fixes most likely to improve trust, visibility, and conversion.

### Pro

$9/month

For teams managing multiple websites and every AI channel.

## Layzr.ai Privacy Policy
# Privacy Policy

[Take me back this is boring](https://www.layzr.ai/)

Last updated: May 16, 2025

At Layzr.ai, we respect your privacy and are committed to protecting the personal information you share with us. This policy explains how we collect, use, and secure your information. By accessing and using our platform, you agree to the terms outlined in this Privacy Policy.

## Information Collection

We do not collect passwords or sensitive account information as all user authentication is managed by Google and Firebase. The only personal information we collect is your name and email address, which are necessary for providing feedback and updates regarding our services.

## Use of Information

The information we collect is solely used to provide the services available on layzr.ai, including support and responding to your requests. We also use your email to communicate important updates and news about our services. We will not sell, distribute, or lease your personal information to third parties.

## Data Security

We are committed to ensuring the security of your personal information. We use reputable services offered by Google and Firebase to manage authentication and protect your data from unauthorized access, disclosure, alteration, or destruction.

## Cookies and Tracking Technologies

Our platform may use cookies and other tracking technologies to enhance user experience and analyze website traffic. These tools help us improve our services and provide more personalized interaction.

## Third-Party Services

We may employ third-party companies and individuals to facilitate our services, such as hosting, database management, and customer service. These third parties have access only to your information that is necessary to perform these tasks on our behalf and are obligated not to disclose or use it for any other purpose. A current list of key subprocessors and their roles is available on our [Subprocessors](https://www.layzr.ai/legal/subprocessors) page.

## Compliance with Laws

We will disclose your personal information where required to do so by law or subpoena or if we believe that such action is necessary to comply with the law and the reasonable requests of law enforcement or to protect the security or integrity of our Service.

## Changes to This Privacy Policy

We may update our Privacy Policy from time to time. We will notify you of any changes by posting the new Privacy Policy on this page. We advise you to review this Privacy Policy periodically for any changes. Changes to this Privacy Policy are effective when they are posted on this page.

## Contact Us

If you have any questions about this Privacy Policy, please contact us via the contact methods provided on our platform.

## Acknowledgement

By accessing or using our platform, you acknowledge that you have read, understood, and agree to be bound by the terms of this Privacy Policy.

\## Frequently Asked Questions

\### What is an AI website audit?

\### An AI website audit is a fast, automated review of your website or landing page. Layzr uses AI to analyze design, layout, messaging, SEO, and trust signals—all within seconds.

\### How does Layzr audit my landing page?

\### Just enter your URL. Layzr captures a screenshot, analyzes the layout, content, and structure using AI, and returns instant feedback to help you improve clarity and conversions.

\### Can AI really review my website like a human would?

\### AI isn’t great at writing original content, but it’s very good at spotting patterns. And landing pages are all about patterns—like layout, call-to-actions, and trust signals. That’s where Layzr works best. It reviews your page like a trained expert and gives feedback based on what actually matters to users.

\### What kinds of sites is Layzr best for?

\### Layzr works best for SaaS homepages, landing pages, product pages, and marketing sites—any page where first impressions matter and conversions are key.

\### How can I optimize my website with AI?

\### With Layzr, you can instantly spot confusing layouts, missing CTAs, weak trust signals, and poor hierarchy. You’ll get actionable suggestions in plain English.

\### How fast is the AI website audit?

\### Layzr’s AI website audit is lightning fast. Just enter your URL, and within seconds, you’ll get a detailed report on your site’s design, layout, and messaging.

\### Does Layzr improve SEO or just design?

\### Both. Layzr reviews on-page SEO like headings and metadata, but also design clarity, trust signals, and call-to-action visibility—all of which impact rankings and conversions.

\### Can I ask the AI questions after the audit?

\### Yes. After the audit, you can chat with Layzr’s AI to dive deeper into the feedback or ask specific questions about how to improve your site.

\# Shopify Website Audit Service

A full audit of your Shopify site is essential if you're serious about growth. SEO, structure, mobile usability—all of it needs regular checks to stay ahead.

\## Why This Matters for Shopify

Shopify users often deal with platform-specific limitations or quirks. Whether it's bloated plugins, rigid themes, or hidden performance drains, these affect how your site ranks and converts.

\## Common Mistakes on Shopify

\- Relying too heavily on third-party apps or plugins that slow down load times
\- Neglecting image optimization or lazy loading
\- Using default Shopify themes without proper structure
\- Skipping mobile responsiveness testing
\- Missing alt tags, meta titles, or page hierarchy issues

\## How Layzr Helps

Layzr runs a custom-tailored audit for Shopify websites. We scan for performance issues, SEO flaws, and layout inefficiencies. You’ll get a prioritized list of improvements and real data backing every suggestion.

\[Audit your Shopify site now →\](https://www.layzr.ai/login)

A full audit of your Framer site is essential if you're serious about growth. SEO, structure, mobile usability—all of it needs regular checks to stay ahead.

## Why This Matters for Framer

Framer users often deal with platform-specific limitations or quirks. Whether it's bloated plugins, rigid themes, or hidden performance drains, these affect how your site ranks and converts.

## Common Mistakes on Framer

- Relying too heavily on third-party apps or plugins that slow down load times
- Neglecting image optimization or lazy loading
- Using default Framer themes without proper structure
- Skipping mobile responsiveness testing
- Missing alt tags, meta titles, or page hierarchy issues

## How Layzr Helps

Layzr runs a custom-tailored audit for Framer websites. We scan for performance issues, SEO flaws, and layout inefficiencies. You’ll get a prioritized list of improvements and real data backing every suggestion.

# Seo Shopify Website Audit

A full audit of your Shopify site is essential if you're serious about growth. SEO, structure, mobile usability—all of it needs regular checks to stay ahead.

## Why This Matters for Shopify

Shopify users often deal with platform-specific limitations or quirks. Whether it's bloated plugins, rigid themes, or hidden performance drains, these affect how your site ranks and converts.

## Common Mistakes on Shopify

- Relying too heavily on third-party apps or plugins that slow down load times
- Neglecting image optimization or lazy loading
- Using default Shopify themes without proper structure
- Skipping mobile responsiveness testing
- Missing alt tags, meta titles, or page hierarchy issues

## How Layzr Helps

Layzr runs a custom-tailored audit for Shopify websites. We scan for performance issues, SEO flaws, and layout inefficiencies. You’ll get a prioritized list of improvements and real data backing every suggestion.

[Audit your Shopify site now →](https://www.layzr.ai/)

## Why This Matters for Squarespace

Squarespace users often deal with platform-specific limitations or quirks. Whether it's bloated plugins, rigid themes, or hidden performance drains, these affect how your site ranks and converts.

- Neglecting image optimization or lazy loading
- Using default Squarespace themes without proper structure
- Skipping mobile responsiveness testing

Next.js users often deal with platform-specific limitations or quirks. Whether it's bloated plugins, rigid themes, or hidden performance drains, these affect how your site ranks and converts.
